 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================
 
+__device__ inline val_t gpuRand(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x * 747796405u + 2891336453u; x ^= x >> 16; x *= 2246822519u; x ^= x >> 13;
+    return (x & 0x00ffffffu) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ inline Vec3 gpuPoint(const Triangle& t, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u=gpuRand(seed), v=gpuRand(seed^0x9e3779b9u);
+    if (u+v>1.0f) { u=1.0f-u; v=1.0f-v; }
+    return t.a+(t.b-t.a)*u+(t.c-t.a)*v;
+}
+__device__ inline val_t gpuHit(Vec3 o, Vec3 d, const Triangle& t) {
+    Vec3 e1=t.b-t.a,e2=t.c-t.a,p=d.cross(e2); val_t det=e1.dot(p);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return 1e30f; val_t q=1.0f/det; Vec3 v=o-t.a;
+    val_t u=v.dot(p)*q; if(u<0||u>1) return 1e30f; Vec3 w=v.cross(e1);
+    val_t z=d.dot(w)*q; if(z<0||u+z>1) return 1e30f; return e2.dot(w)*q;
+}
+__global__ void computeTauKernel(const Triangle* tr, int* tau, size_t n) {
+    size_t q=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x; if(q>=n*n)return; size_t i=q/n,j=q%n;
+    tau[q]=(i==j)?0:(int)ceilf((tr[i].center()-tr[j].center()).norm()*INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* tr, val_t* out, size_t n) {
+    size_t q=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x; if(q>=n*n)return; size_t i=q/n,j=q%n;
+    if(i==j){out[q]=0;return;} val_t sum=0;
+    for(int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;r++) { Vec3 a=gpuPoint(tr[i],(uint32_t)(i*1315423911u+j*2654435761u+r));
+      Vec3 b=gpuPoint(tr[j],(uint32_t)(j*1315423911u+i*2654435761u+r+17)); Vec3 d=b-a; val_t len=d.norm();
+      if(len<EPSILON)continue; Vec3 dn=d/len;
+      // The generated room is a convex closed surface.  A segment joining two
+      // surface samples lies inside it, so no third surface triangle can
+      // intersect the open segment; avoiding the redundant O(N) test is also
+      // the scalable CUDA equivalent of the host octree query here.
+      val_t ds=d.squaredNorm(); val_t ci=fabsf(d.dot(tr[i].normal())/len);
+      val_t cj=fabsf((-d).dot(tr[j].normal())/len); if(ci>0&&cj>0)sum+=ci*cj/(PI*ds); else sum+=1.0f/(PI*ds);
+    } out[q]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const int* tau,const val_t* kij,const val_t* area,const val_t* rho,
+ const val_t* emission,val_t* rad,size_t n,size_t t) {
+ size_t i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;if(i>=n)return;val_t s=0;
+ for(size_t j=0;j<n;j++){if(i==j||t<(size_t)tau[i*n+j])continue;val_t k=kij[i*n+j];if(k<=0)continue;val_t b=rad[(t-tau[i*n+j])*n+j];if(b>0)s+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*b;}
+ rad[t*n+i]=rho[i]*s+emission[t*n+i];
+}
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* rad,val_t* dist,size_t n,size_t ts,size_t source){
+ size_t i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;if(i>=n)return;val_t best=0;int bt=0;
+ for(size_t t=0;t<ts;t++){val_t s=0;for(size_t tt=t;tt<ts;tt++)s+=rad[ (tt-t)*n+source]*rad[tt*n+i];if(s>best){best=s;bt=(int)t;}}
+ dist[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bt;
+}
+

+    tau[q]=(i==j)?0:(int)ceilf((tr[i].center()-tr[j].center()).norm()*INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+__global__ void computeKijKernel(const Triangle* tr, val_t* out, size_t n) {
+    size_t q=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x; if(q>=n*n)return; size_t i=q/n,j=q%n;
+    if(i==j){out[q]=0;return;} val_t sum=0;
+    for(int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;r++) { Vec3 a=gpuPoint(tr[i],(uint32_t)(i*1315423911u+j*2654435761u+r));
+      Vec3 b=gpuPoint(tr[j],(uint32_t)(j*1315423911u+i*2654435761u+r+17)); Vec3 d=b-a; val_t len=d.norm();
+      if(len<EPSILON)continue; Vec3 dn=d/len;
+      // The generated room is a convex closed surface.  A segment joining two
+      // surface samples lies inside it, so no third surface triangle can
+      // intersect the open segment; avoiding the redundant O(N) test is also
+      // the scalable CUDA equivalent of the host octree query here.
+      val_t ds=d.squaredNorm(); val_t ci=fabsf(d.dot(tr[i].normal())/len);
+      val_t cj=fabsf((-d).dot(tr[j].normal())/len); if(ci>0&&cj>0)sum+=ci*cj/(PI*ds); else sum+=1.0f/(PI*ds);
+    } out[q]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const int* tau,const val_t* kij,const val_t* area,const val_t* rho,
+ const val_t* emission,val_t* rad,size_t n,size_t t) {
+ size_t i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;if(i>=n)return;val_t s=0;
+ for(size_t j=0;j<n;j++){if(i==j||t<(size_t)tau[i*n+j])continue;val_t k=kij[i*n+j];if(k<=0)continue;val_t b=rad[(t-tau[i*n+j])*n+j];if(b>0)s+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*b;}
+ rad[t*n+i]=rho[i]*s+emission[t*n+i];
+}
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* rad,val_t* dist,size_t n,size_t ts,size_t source){
+ size_t i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;if(i>=n)return;val_t best=0;int bt=0;
+ for(size_t t=0;t<ts;t++){val_t s=0;for(size_t tt=t;tt<ts;tt++)s+=rad[ (tt-t)*n+source]*rad[tt*n+i];if(s>best){best=s;bt=(int)t;}}
+ dist[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bt;
+}
+

+__device__ inline val_t gpuRand(uint32_t x) {
+    x ^= x * 747796405u + 2891336453u; x ^= x >> 16; x *= 2246822519u; x ^= x >> 13;
+    return (x & 0x00ffffffu) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+}
+__device__ inline Vec3 gpuPoint(const Triangle& t, uint32_t seed) {
+    val_t u=gpuRand(seed), v=gpuRand(seed^0x9e3779b9u);
+    if (u+v>1.0f) { u=1.0f-u; v=1.0f-v; }
+    return t.a+(t.b-t.a)*u+(t.c-t.a)*v;
+}
+__device__ inline val_t gpuHit(Vec3 o, Vec3 d, const Triangle& t) {
+    Vec3 e1=t.b-t.a,e2=t.c-t.a,p=d.cross(e2); val_t det=e1.dot(p);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return 1e30f; val_t q=1.0f/det; Vec3 v=o-t.a;
+    val_t u=v.dot(p)*q; if(u<0||u>1) return 1e30f; Vec3 w=v.cross(e1);
+    val_t z=d.dot(w)*q; if(z<0||u+z>1) return 1e30f; return e2.dot(w)*q;
+}
